# Household hh_s3 — 28 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, bedroom_3, entry, kitchen, living, storage.

Residents:

- **Felix** (resident_1): works an afternoon-to-night shift; untidy (tidiness 0.39); punctual (jitter scale 0.78); rarely forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.23); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: movies, plants. Chores they take on: fixing, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Aisha** (resident_2): works from home; untidy (tidiness 0.33); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.57); rarely forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.48); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: friends, meditation, movies. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Ines** (resident_3): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.92); average timing (jitter scale 1.22); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.86); sleeps in bedroom_3, works at the bedroom_3 desk. Hobbies: baking, reading_living, running. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.

## Day 1 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d1`: Aisha hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:37–16:34).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d1`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:27–15:57).
- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d1`: Felix turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).

## Day 3 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d3`: Aisha hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:28–17:38).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d3`: Ines is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:58–15:28).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_1:d3`: Felix gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:51–20:36).

## Day 5 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d5`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:02–16:01).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d5`: Aisha gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:07–19:52).

## Day 9 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_3:d9`: Ines hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:14–16:10).
- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_3:d9`: Ines gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (20:49–21:34).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d9`: Felix can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:51–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Aisha is distracted (0.79): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Aisha has low energy (0.20): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Felix energy 0.50, hurriedness 0.27, distraction 0.14; Aisha energy 0.20, hurriedness 0.36, distraction 0.79; Ines energy 0.54, hurriedness 0.38, distraction 0.48

## Day 13 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d13`: Felix is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:20–16:50).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d13`: Felix can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:57–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d13`: Aisha is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (14:49–16:49).
- `distracted:resident_2`: Aisha is distracted (0.82): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `running_late:resident_3`: Ines is running late all day (0.79): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Felix energy 0.62, hurriedness 0.66, distraction 0.43; Aisha energy 0.40, hurriedness 0.04, distraction 0.82; Ines energy 0.59, hurriedness 0.79, distraction 0.54
